# NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) Mathematics - Complex Number

## Topic 3: Conjugate, Modulus and Argument

**Total Questions:** 66

### Q1. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For a complex number <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math>, if one root of the equation <math><msup><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>Z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and its other root is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>α</mi></math>, then the value of <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>α</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>  
<math><mi>α</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>  
<math><mi>α</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi><mi>i</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>α</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></math>  
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi></math>  
<math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>  
<math><msup><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>  
<math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

</details>

### Q2. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the complex numbers <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math> and <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> are conjugate of each other, then the number of values of <math><mi>x</mi></math> in the interval <math><mfenced open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to (where, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>i</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> and <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi><mo> </mo><mo>&amp; </mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi><mo> </mo><mo>&amp; </mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>x</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi><mo> </mo><mo>&amp; </mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> (Which is impossible)
Hence, no value of <math><mi>x</mi></math> exists

</details>

### Q3. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>B</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi>C</mi><mo>(</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>)</mo></math> be complex numbers satisfying the equation <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and also satisfying the relation <math><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>. Then <math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1.75**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>B</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>C</mi></math> lie on the unit circle centered at the origin. Also,
<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>1.0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p>i.e. the line segment joining <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> and origin<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>D</mi></mfenced></math> bisects the line segment joining <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>E</mi></math></p><p>Also, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>D</mi><mi>E</mi><mo>:</mo><mi>E</mi><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>:</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>D</mi><mi>E</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>K</mi></math> and <math><mi>E</mi><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>K</mi></math>
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/c3acb540-bf0a-47b3-9753-0e7a2dc73c03.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:2.02014in;height:1.66042in" />
Now, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>D</mi><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>K</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>K</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>D</mi><mi>E</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>
From <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#9651;</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>E</mi><mi>D</mi><mo>,</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext mathvariant="italic">BE</mtext><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>9</mn><mn>16</mn></mfrac></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>75</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q4. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> are two distinct complex numbers satisfying the relation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><msubsup><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>-</mo><msubsup><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><msub><mover><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo></mover><mn>1</mn></msub><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><msub><mover><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo></mover><mn>2</mn></msub><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mover><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo></mover><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mover><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo></mover><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">arg</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">arg</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>, then the least possible value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to (where, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi></math> are integers)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><msubsup><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>-</mo><msubsup><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><msub><mover><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo></mover><mn>1</mn></msub><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><msub><mover><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo></mover><mn>2</mn></msub><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mover><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo></mover><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mover><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo></mover><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msubsup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>-</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msubsup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>+</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#175;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8869;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#175;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">arg</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>&#177;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
i.e. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo></math>
The minimum value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> (when <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>)</p>

</details>

### Q5. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For a complex number <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi></math>, if <math><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>i</mi></math> and <math><mi>z</mi></math> does not lie in the first quadrant, then (where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>i</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">arg</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">arg</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Put <math><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>y</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>i</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
Case <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> if <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>y</mi></math>, then <math><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>2</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
Case <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> if <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>&#981;</mi></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mi>z</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math></p>

</details>

### Q6. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For a complex number <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math>, if <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>arg</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>Z</mi></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>, then the value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><msqrt><mn>18</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><msqrt><mn>12</mn></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let, <math><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math> then,</p><p> <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>32</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>32</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>14</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></math></p>

</details>

### Q7. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the locus of the complex number <math><mi>z</mi></math> given by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>ar</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math> is an arc of a circle, then the length of the arc is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>arg</mi><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>Let, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>y</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Hence, the given locus is a circle with centre <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math> units
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/85ad140b-a726-4e87-880c-620cb638854c.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:1.79375in;height:1.52014in" />
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi></mi></math> Length of the arc of the circle is <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units</p>

</details>

### Q8. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For a complex number <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math>, if all the roots of the equation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>Z</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><msup><mi>Z</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>Z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> are unimodular, then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>3</mn></math> and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≤</mo><mn>3</mn></math> and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>3</mn></math> and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≤</mo><mn>3</mn></math> &amp; <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≤</mo><mn>3</mn></math> &amp; <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
Now, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>≤</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≤</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≤</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
Also, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>×</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>×</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q9. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>m</mi></math> and <math><mi>M</mi></math> denotes the minimum and maximum value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>i</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>, then the value of <math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>M</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>m</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>17</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>34</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>51</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>16</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>16</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> represents a circle with centre at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math> unit</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/99f94c8e-fa5e-40bc-a3ad-4e725b187393.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:2.68056in;height:2.06042in" /></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> represents twice the distance from the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Hence from the diagram,</p><p> <math><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi><mi>D</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>C</mi><mi>D</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>17</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>17</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mi>M</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>B</mi><mi>D</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>C</mi><mi>D</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><msqrt><mn>17</mn></msqrt><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>17</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>M</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>m</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>4</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q10. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If a complex number <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></math> lie on a circle of radius <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math> units, then the complex number <math> <mrow> <mi>&#969;</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mtext>z</mtext> </mrow> </math> will always lie on a circle of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi></math> units, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **2**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let us assume that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></math> lies on a circle with centre <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math> (fixed point) and radius <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math> units.

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#8658; &#160;&#160;</mo> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

Now, <math> <mrow> <mi>&#969;</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>&#8658;</mo> <mi>&#969;</mi> <mo>+</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>=</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mtext>z</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#8658; &#160;&#160;</mo> <mi>&#969;</mi> <mo>+</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mn>4</mn> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <mn>4</mn> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> </math>

Now, taking modulus on both sides, we get,

<math> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <mi>&#969;</mi> <mo>+</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mn>4</mn> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>&#8658;</mo> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <mi>&#969;</mi> <mo>+</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mn>4</mn> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </math>

Locus of <math> <mrow> <mi>&#969;</mi> </mrow> </math> represents the circle having centre <math> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mn>4</mn> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math> and radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math> units.

</details>

### Q11. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>z</mi></math> is a complex number satisfying <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>R</mi><mi>e</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>I</mi><mi>m</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> cannot be

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>17</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math><msqrt><mn>8</mn></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>y</mi></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
Minimum value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
Maximum value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
So, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> can’t be <math><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></math>
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/846c6b61-9c3a-4699-a0b8-58c380a00218.jpeg" />

</details>

---

### Q12. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The minimum value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>8</mn><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> exists, when <math><mi>Z</mi></math> is equal to (where, <math><mi>Z</mi></math> is a complex number)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1.5</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given expression is
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>8</mn><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
Now, minimum <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to <math><mn>1</mn></math>, when <math><mi>Z</mi></math> lies on the line segment joining <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>.</mo></math>
And, minimum <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to <math><mn>0</mn></math>, when <math><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> Minimum <math><mn>6</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to <math><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> at <math><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q13. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>≠</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></math> be any complex number such that <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> is a purely imaginary number, then, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** any non-zero real number other than <math><mn>1</mn></math> .
- **(B)** a purely imaginary number.
- **(C)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** any non-zero real number

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) any non-zero real number**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let <math><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> is a purely imaginary number
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math> is a purely imaginary
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math> is purely imaginary
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math>
 <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><mn>1</mn></math> so <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>  (from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mfenced></math> and since, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></math> won&rsquo;t be an imaginary number)

</details>

### Q14. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi>B</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> are two vertices of a square <math><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi><mi>D</mi></math> (taken in anticlockwise order) in a complex plane, then the value of <math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> (where <math><mi>C</mi></math> is <math><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><mi>D</mi></math> is <math><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math> ) is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>8</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>12</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>12</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>i</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>i</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>i</mi></math>
and <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>i</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>i</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>29</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>17</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q15. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi></math> is a complex number satisfying <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math>, then the sum of the absolute values of the imaginary parts of the roots of the equation is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> <em>or</em> <math><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><mi>i</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi>i</mi></math>
Hence, the required sum is <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

---

### Q16. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><msup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>, then for a complex number <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math> the minimum value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> occurs at

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/6e7c8cb8-a751-4c3a-b041-9ed1e91d99a7.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:1.97986in;height:1.26042in" />
The minimum value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> occurs at points on the line segment joining <math><mi>O</mi></math> and <math><mi>A</mi></math>
Similarly, the minimum value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> occurs at a point on the line segment joining <math><mi>B</mi></math> and <math><mi>C</mi></math>.
<math><mo>⇒</mo></math> Minimum value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> occurs at a point on the intersection of <math><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math></p><p>Hence, the minimum value occurs at <math><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q17. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The minimum value of the expression <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to (where, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi></math> is a complex number)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1.5</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given expression is,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
Now, minimum <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to <math><mn>1</mn></math>
when <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi></math> lies on the line segment joining <math><mn>1</mn></math> &amp; <math><mn>2</mn></math></p><p>and minimum <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is equals to <math><mn>0</mn></math>, when <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> Minimum of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to <math><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q18. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi></math> be non-zero complex numbers such that <math><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>.</mo></math> If <math><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi></math> varies, then the perimeter of the locus of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>8</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math> units
- **(B)** <math><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math> units
- **(C)** <math><mn>8</mn></math> units
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn></math> units

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>8</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math> units**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>=</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo></mover><mo> </mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Now, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
Let, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></math>, then we get,
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
which represents a square of side length equal to <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/091aa721-604d-4478-a296-df4da708699d.png" style="width:1.65347in;height:1.47986in" />
<math><mo>⇒</mo></math> The perimeter of the locus is <math><mn>8</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math> units

</details>

### Q19. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>iz</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>|</mo></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>iz</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mfrac><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>iz</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>iz</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></math> or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></math>

Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>|</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

### Q20. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> are the points on the argand plane which lie on the circle with equation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math> (where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math> is the centre of the circle). If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">arg</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to (where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>arg</mi><mi>Z</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Here <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math>
Therefore, center coincides with the circumcentre
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> Triangle is equilateral <math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
Clearly, <math><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> either lie in the second or third quadrant</p><p>So the centre <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math> also lies in the second or third quadrant.
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math> can be <math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mtext>i</mtext><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mtext>i</mtext></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>arg</mi><mfenced><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q21. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>z</mi><mi>i</mi></msub></math> (where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>i</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mn>6</mn></math>) be the roots of the equation <math><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, then <math><mrow><msubsup><mo stretchy="false">&#8721;</mo><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>8</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>1</mn><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>1</mn><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let, <math><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> or <math><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#177;</mo><mi>i</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><mn>1</mn></math> or <math><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#177;</mo><mi>i</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>z</mi></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>z</mi></mfenced><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msubsup><mo>&#8721;</mo><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>6</mn></msubsup><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><msub><mi>z</mi><mi>i</mi></msub></mfenced><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math>

</details>

### Q22. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For two non-zero complex numbers <math><mi>A</mi></math> and <math><mi>B</mi></math>, if <math><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#175;</mo></mover></math> and <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#175;</mo></mover></math>, then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>A</mi></math> is purely real
- **(B)** <math><mi>B</mi></math> is purely real
- **(C)** <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#175;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mi>A</mi></math> and <math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#175;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#175;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>-</mo><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#175;</mo></mover></mrow></mfrac></math>
And <math><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#175;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>-</mo><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#175;</mo></mover></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#175;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>-</mo><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#175;</mo></mover></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>-</mo><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#175;</mo></mover></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover><mi>A</mi><mo>&#175;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>A</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p>

</details>
