### Q23. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>z</mi></math> and <math><mi>w</mi></math> are complex numbers satisfying <math><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mo>¯</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>w</mi></mrow><mo>¯</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>amp</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mi>w</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>amp</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>w</mi></mfenced></math> is equal to (where, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>amp</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>w</mi></mfenced><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>w</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>w</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math> or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>w</mi></math> or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>z</mi><mi>w</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>i</mi></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>w</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>i</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
also <math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mi>w</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>w</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
Adding <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> we get,</p><p><math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Also, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>w</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q24. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>n</mi></math> be a positive integer and a complex number with unit modulus is a solution of the equation <math><msup><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>Z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi></math> can be

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>87</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>97</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>104</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>222</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>104</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let, <math><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> satisfies <math><msup><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>Z</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> also satisfies <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> also satisfies <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> &hellip; <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Now, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> &hellip; <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
From <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> &amp; <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>,
we get, <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msubsup></math> must be the point of intersection of
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> &amp; <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>Z</mi><mi>n</mi></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mn>1</mn><mi>n</mi></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi>&#969;</mi></math> or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>&#969;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math> {where, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#969;</mi></math> is non-real cube root of unity}
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> can be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#969;</mi></math> or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>&#969;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math> </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>n</mi></math> is of the form of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q25. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>Let <math><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> be the <math><msup><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi><mi>h</mi></mrow></msup></math> roots of unity which are ends of a line segment that subtend a right angle at the origin. Then <math><mi>n</mi></math> must be of the form</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>4</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>4</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn><mi>k</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>4</mn><mi>k</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>r</mi><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>r</mi><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi>,  </mi><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>, </mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>, </mo><mi></mi><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mo>, </mo><mi mathvariant="italic"> n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

Let, <math><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi> </mi><mi>i</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>

And, <math><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math> .

Then, <math> <mrow> <mo>∠</mo><msub> <mi>z</mi> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mi>O</mi><msub> <mi>z</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>p</mi><mo> </mo><mfenced> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi>z</mi> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mi>z</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>p</mi><mo> </mo><mfenced> <mrow> <msub> <mi>z</mi> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>−</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>p</mi><mo> </mo><mfenced> <mrow> <msub> <mi>z</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> (Given)

<math><mo>∴</mo></math> <math><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>×</mo></math> integer, so <math><mi>n</mi></math> is of the form <math><mn>4</mn><mi>k</mi></math>.

</details>

### Q26. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

The minimum value of the expression <math><mi>E</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> (where <math><mi>z</mi></math> is a complex number) is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **30**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let <math><mi>z</mi><mi></mi><mo>=</mo><mi></mi><mi>x</mi><mi></mi><mo>+</mo><mi></mi><mi>i</mi><mi>y</mi></math>
<math> <mrow> <mi>E</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>z</mi><mover accent="true"> <mi>z</mi> <mo>¯</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo><mfenced> <mrow> <mi>z</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mfenced> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>z</mi> <mo>¯</mo> </mover> <mo>−</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced> <mrow> <mi>z</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow> </mfenced><mfenced> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>z</mi> <mo>¯</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mover accent="true"> <mi>z</mi> <mo>¯</mo> </mover> <mo>−</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced> <mrow> <mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>z</mi> <mo>¯</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mfenced> <mrow> <mi>z</mi><mo>−</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>z</mi> <mo>¯</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced><mi>i</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>36</mn></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>z</mi> <mo>¯</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>;</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>−</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>z</mi> <mo>¯</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>45</mn></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>15</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mi>E</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Hence, <math><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>30</mn></math> when <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi></mi></math> and <math><mi>y</mi><mi></mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> i.e., <math><mi>z</mi><mi></mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi></mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi></math>

</details>

### Q27. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>O</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0,0</mn></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mn>3,0</mn></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3,2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the minimum value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>z</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> occurs at the (where, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi></math> is a complex number)

**Options:**

- **(A)** point of intersection of <math><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></math> and <math><mi>C</mi><mi>O</mi></math>
- **(B)** point of intersection of <math><mi>A</mi><mi>C</mi></math> and <math><mi>B</mi><mi>O</mi></math>
- **(C)** point of intersection of <math><mi>C</mi><mi>B</mi></math> and <math><mi>A</mi><mi>O</mi></math>
- **(D)** Mean of <math><mi>O</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>B</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>C</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) point of intersection of <math><mi>C</mi><mi>B</mi></math> and <math><mi>A</mi><mi>O</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/19fb4e5d-2fc5-4dec-a483-0f52ef111604.png" style="width:2.46042in;height:1.58681in" />
The minimum value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>z</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> occurs at points on the line segment joining <math><mi>O</mi></math> &amp; <math><mi>A</mi></math>
Similarly, the minimum value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
occurs at points on the line segment joining <math><mi>B</mi></math> &amp; <math><mi>C</mi><mo>.</mo></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo></math> The minimum value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>z</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
occurs at point of intersection of <math><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi></math> &amp; <math><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi><mo>.</mo></math>

</details>

### Q28. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>ω</mi></math> is the non-real cube root of unity, then the number of ordered pairs of integers <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>, such that <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>ω</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>, is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **6**

**Detailed Solution:**

We have, <math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>ω</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>ω</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>ω</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>ω</mi><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>ω</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>…</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s, </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ω</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
When <math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> then <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
When <math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> then <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Hence, <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> i.e., <math><mn>6</mn></math> ordered pairs.

</details>

### Q29. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi></math> are two non-zero complex numbers such that <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>, then the value of <math><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>–</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>–</mo><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>5</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac bevelled="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>  
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></math>  
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>  
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>  
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>  
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>  
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q30. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For a complex number <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi></math>, If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>arg</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>z</mi></mfenced><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>arg</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> is (here <math><msup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>i.e. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#969;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>arg</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>z</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q31. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi></math> be a complex number satisfying <math><mo>|</mo><mi>z</mi><mi> </mi><mo>–</mo><mn>4</mn><mi> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi> </mi><mn>8</mn><mi>i</mi><mo>|</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then the least and the greatest value of <math><mo>|</mo><mi>z</mi><mi> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi> </mi><mn>2</mn><mo>|</mo></math> are respectively (where <math><mi>i</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt></math> )

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mn>7</mn></math> and <math> <mn>16</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math> <mn>8</mn></math> and <math> <mn>17</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math> <mn>6</mn> </math> and <math> <mn>14</mn> </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mn>5</mn></math> and <math> <mn>13</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math> <mn>6</mn> </math> and <math> <mn>14</mn> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mo>|</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>|</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>|</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>i</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>i</mi><mo>|</mo></math>

<math><mo>|</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>|</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>|</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>i</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>i</mi><mo>|</mo></math>

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8804;</mo><mo>|</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>i</mi><mo>|</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>|</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>i</mi><mo>|</mo></math>

<math><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>14</mn></math>

<math><mo>|</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>|</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>|</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>i</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>i</mi><mo>|</mo></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8805;</mo><mo>|</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>i</mi><mo>|</mo><mo>-</mo><mo>|</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>i</mi><mo>|</mo></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8805;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>

</details>

### Q32. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For a complex number <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math>, if <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then the range of the principle argument of <math><mi>Z</mi></math> is (where principle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>arg</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>Z</mi></mfenced><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> )

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo></math> locus of <math><mi>Z</mi></math> is the line segment joining <math><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi></math> with <math><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi></math>

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/fd7a32bf-90b4-4617-8b44-4ebd9eb889eb.png" style="width:1.97986in;height:1.46042in" />
<math><mo>⇒</mo></math> minimum arg <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> &amp; maximum arg <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo></math> principal arg <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q33. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let the locus of any point <math><mi>P</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> in the argand plane is <math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>.</mo></math> If <math><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math> is the origin, then the value of <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"/><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/648909.PNG" style="width: 220px; height: 186px;" /></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>r</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>r</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>O</mi><mi>C</mi><mo>+</mo></math> radius <math><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
Required value <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q34. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></math>, then which of the following is true? (where, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Here, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>13</mn></msqrt></math>
Hence, <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> lie on a circle with center <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>13</mn></msqrt></math> as shown
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/c085a59b-b3ee-4088-9447-58c8c76c3c14.png" style="width: 220px; height: 210px;" />
Now, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q35. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

The number of distinct complex number(s) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi></math>, such that <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math> is purely imaginary, is/are equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **6**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#952;</mi></math>
Since, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></math> is purely imaginary
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>&#177;</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Possible value of <math><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>30</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msup><mrow><mn>150</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msup><mrow><mn>90</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msup><mrow><mn>270</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msup><mrow><mn>210</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msup><mrow><mn>330</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>.</mo></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q36. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If arg <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>10</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>π</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math> (where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi></math> is a complex number), then the perimeter of the locus of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>13</mn></msqrt><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>13</mn></msqrt><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>13</mn></msqrt><mi>π</mi></math> units
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msqrt><mn>26</mn></msqrt></math> units

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msqrt><mn>26</mn></msqrt></math> units**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let <math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4,2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, <math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>10,6</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi>C</mi></math> is the center of locus of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi></math> (which is a circle)
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/012702c5-2091-4d45-a5f9-89cedd1a68b3.jpeg" style="width:1.55833in;height:1.21667in" />
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>C</mi><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>C</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8736;</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>C</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>C</mi><mi>B</mi></math> is right-angled isosceles triangle
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>r</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>r</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>36</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>52</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>r</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>26</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>26</mn></msqrt></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> perimeter<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msqrt><mn>26</mn></msqrt></math> units

</details>

---

### Q37. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>y</mi></math> and <math><mi>w</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>v</mi></math> be two complex numbers, such that <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>w</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>w</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo></math> Then, the number of ordered pairs <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>w</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to (where, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>i</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **8**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let, <math><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></msup></math> and <math><mi>w</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></msup></math> <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>w</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi><mn>2</mn><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><mi>&#960;</mi></math>
For <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>,</mo></math> we have <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>pairs</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>of</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
For <math><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> we have <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>pairs</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>of</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
For <math><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>,</mo></math> we have <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>pairs</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>of</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q38. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>i</mi></math>. If the real part of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mover><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mover><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mover><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn></math>, then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to (where, <math><msup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msup><mrow><mo>|</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>|</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>25</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>25</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mrow><mo>|</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>|</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mo>|</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>|</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mo>|</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>|</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>25</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>25</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>(</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mrow><mo>|</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>|</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

### Q39. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For a complex number <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math>, if <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≤</mo><mn>2</mn></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>i</mi></math>, then the maximum value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>Z</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> is (where, <math><msup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>7</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>13</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>7</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>12</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>7</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><msqrt><mn>34</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>7</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>Z</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>i</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8804;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>i</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8804;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><msup><mn>4</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math>

</details>

### Q40. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A complex number <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></math> is said to be unimodular if <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>|</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>. Let, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> are complex numbers such that <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2z</mn><msub><mtext></mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <msub> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>¯</mo> </mover> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math> is unimodular and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> is not unimodular, then the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> lies on a

**Options:**

- **(A)** circle of radius <math> <mrow> <msqrt> <mn>2</mn> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** straight line parallel to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math>-axis
- **(C)** straight line parallel to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi></math>-axis
- **(D)** circle of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) circle of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given, <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2z</mn><msub><mtext></mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <msub> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>&#175;</mo> </mover> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>  is unimodular

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#8658;&#160;</mo> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn>&#160;<msub><mtext>z</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <msub> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>&#175;</mo> </mover> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </math>

<math><mo>&#8658;&#160;</mo> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>-</mo>&#160;<msub><mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <msub> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>&#175;</mo> </mover> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

Squaring both the sides, we get,

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2z</mn><msub><mtext></mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>-</mo>&#160;<msub><mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <msub> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>&#175;</mo> </mover> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mo>&#8658;&#160;</mo><mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>&#175;</mo> </mover> </mrow> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>&#175;</mo> </mover> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <msub> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>&#175;</mo> </mover> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>&#175;</mo> </mover> </mrow> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mo>&#8757;&#160;</mo> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>z</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>z&#160;</mtext> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>&#175;</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

<math><mo>&#8658;&#160;</mo> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <msub> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>&#175;</mo> </mover> </mrow> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1&#160;</mn> </msub> <msub> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>&#175;</mo> </mover> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>&#175;</mo> </mover> </mrow> <mn>1&#160;</mn> </msub> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>+</mo> <mn>4</mn> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>2&#160;</mn> </msub> <msub> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>&#175;</mo> </mover> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>&#175;</mo> </mover> </mrow> <mn>1&#160;</mn> </msub> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1&#160;</mn> </msub> <msub> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>&#175;</mo> </mover> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1&#160;</mn> </msub> <msub> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>&#175;</mo> </mover> </mrow> <mn>1&#160;</mn> </msub> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>2&#160;</mn> </msub> <msub> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>z</mtext> <mo>&#175;</mo> </mover> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mo>&#8658;&#160;</mo><mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>+</mo> <mn>4</mn> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2&#160;</mn> </msup> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mo>&#8658;&#160;</mo><mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>-</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mn>4</mn> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>-</mo> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2&#160;</mn> </msup> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math>

<math><mo>&#8658;&#160;</mo> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>-</mo> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;&#160;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|"><msub><mtext>z</mtext><mn>1</mn></msub></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2&#160;&#160;</mn><mtext>or&#160;</mtext><mfenced open="|" close="|"><msub><mtext>z</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p> Given, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> is not unimodular

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#8756;&#160;&#160;</mo> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#8756;</mo> </mrow> </math>  Point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> lies on a circle of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math>.</p>
