### Q41. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For a complex number <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math>, if <math><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msqrt><mi>π</mi></msqrt><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mi>π</mi></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mi>π</mi></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mi>π</mi></msqrt><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>Z</mi></mfenced><mrow><mi>amp</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>Z</mi></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></math> is equal to (where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>amp</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>Z</mi></mfenced><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>π</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>4</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><msup><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msqrt><mi>&#960;</mi></msqrt><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mi>&#960;</mi></msqrt><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mi>&#960;</mi></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mi>&#960;</mi></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mi>&#960;</mi></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>i</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mo>-</mo><msqrt><mi>&#960;</mi></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mn>4</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>i</mi></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>amp</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>Z</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>Z</mi></mfenced><mrow><mi>amp</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>Z</mi></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

</details>

### Q42. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> are <math><mn>3</mn></math> distinct complex numbers such that <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math>, then the value of <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>49</mn></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>15</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let, <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>49</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>
similarly, <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>
and <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>49</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>49</mn></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>

### Q43. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For a complex number <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math>, if the argument of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>i</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> are equal, then the maximum distance of <math><mi>Z</mi></math> from the <math><mi>x</mi></math>-axis is equal to (where, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>i</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math> units
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> units

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> units**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let, <math><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>y</mi></math>
Now, <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>Z</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mi>Z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>y</mi></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>where</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Centre of the circle is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> and radius <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/3ba3c449-1c8b-4b44-950d-e27e44a7794f.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:2.12708in;height:1.27986in" />
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> required value <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units</p>

</details>

---

### Q44. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>ϕ</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>ϕ</mi><mo> </mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>∀</mo><mi>ϕ</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mi>π</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>arg</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>Z</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to (where, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>arg</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>Z</mi><mo>)</mo></math> represents the argument of the complex number <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math> lying in the interval <math><mfenced close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>i</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>ϕ</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>ϕ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>ϕ</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>ϕ</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>ϕ</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>Z</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>ϕ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>ϕ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>ϕ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>ϕ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>ϕ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>ϕ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfrac><mrow><mi>ϕ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>ϕ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>ϕ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>ϕ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>ϕ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>ϕ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfrac><mi>ϕ</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>ϕ</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>ϕ</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
Now, <math><mn>3</mn><mi>ϕ</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇒</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>ϕ</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>∈</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
But, the argument lies in <math><mfenced close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>, hence
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>arg</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>Z</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>ϕ</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>ϕ</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q45. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> are the vertices of a triangle in the argand plane such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">arg</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/d670a7d4-054e-4c42-a94a-6e95a27f65ea.png" style="max-width:"100%"; object-fit:contain" style="width:1.78681in;height:1.46667in" /></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></mfrac><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></msup><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></mfrac><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo></math> purely imaginary number
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q46. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>z</mi></math> is a non-real complex number, then the minimum value of <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>I</mi><mi>m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi>z</mi><mn>5</mn></msup></mrow><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>I</mi><mi>m</mi><mo> </mo><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>5</mn></msup></mfrac></math> is (<math><mi>I</mi><mi>m</mi><mo> </mo><mi>z</mi><mo> </mo><mo>=</mo></math> Imaginary part of <math><mi>z</mi></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let, <math><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>r</mi><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mtext>cos</mtext><mi>θ</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mtext>sin</mtext><mi>θ</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>z</mi> <mn>5</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo><msup> <mi>r</mi> <mn>5</mn> </msup> <mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>5</mn><mi>θ</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mtext>sin</mtext><mn>5</mn><mi>θ</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow> </math>

<math><mi>I</mi><mi>m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>z</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></msup><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>r</mi><mn>5</mn></msup><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>θ</mi></math>

and <math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>I</mi><mi>m</mi><mi> </mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>I</mi><mi>m</mi><mi> </mi><mo>(</mo><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>I</mi><mi>m</mi><mi> </mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi> A </mi></math>(Let)

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>5</mn></msup><mo>⁡</mo><mi>θ</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>θ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>θ</mi><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>⁡</mo><mi>θ</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>θ</mi></mrow><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>5</mn></msup><mo>⁡</mo><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>⁡</mo><mi>θ</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>θ</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>θ</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>θ</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>10</mn></msup><mo>⁡</mo><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>⇒</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mi>d</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow> <mrow> <mi>d</mi><mi>θ</mi></mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mn>5</mn> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mi>sin</mi></mrow> <mn>6</mn> </msup> <mi>θ</mi></mrow> </mfrac> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <mi>sin</mi><mfenced> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow> </math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>π</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>

The minimum value of <math><mi>A</mi></math> will be at  <math><mi>θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>π</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>.</mo></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>5</mn><mfrac><mi>π</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></mrow><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfrac><mi>π</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mn>5</mn></msup></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced><mn>5</mn></msup></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfenced><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

</details>

### Q47. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Two points <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></math> in the argand plane represent the complex numbers <math><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></math> and <math><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>.</mo></math> If <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>, then <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></math> moves on the circle, whose centre and radius are (here, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>6</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo></math> <math><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>,</mo></math> <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>36</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>31</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Centre <math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2,1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Radius <math><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>31</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>

</details>

### Q48. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For the complex number <math><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></math> satisfying the condition <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>, the maximum value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>|</mo></mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mo>|</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>|</mo></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mo>|</mo></mrow><mo>&#8804;</mo><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi></mfrac><mo>|</mo></mrow><mo>&#8804;</mo><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>|</mo></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mo>|</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>|</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>|</mo></mrow></math>

Now, for maximum or minimum value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>|</mo></mrow><mo>,</mo></math> LHS inequality will hold true as it corresponds to the two complex numbers being on opposite sides of origin.</p><p>Hence, <math> <mrow> <mrow><mo>|</mo> <mi>z</mi> <mo>|</mo></mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mfrac> <mn>2</mn> <mrow> <mrow><mo>|</mo> <mi>z</mi> <mo>|</mo></mrow></mrow> </mfrac> <mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> </math> (without loss of generality let LHS &gt; 0)</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mrow><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>|</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>|</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>|</mo></mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mo>|</mo> <mi>z</mi> <mo>|</mo></mrow><mo>&#8804;</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt> <mrow> <mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow> </msqrt> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mo>|</mo> <mi>z</mi> <mo>|</mo></mrow><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt> <mn>3</mn> </msqrt> </mrow> </math></p>

</details>

### Q49. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Re</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to (Where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> are complex numbers)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>14</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>14</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo></math><math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>14</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>Re</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>14</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Re</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Re</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>

### Q50. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For a complex number <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math>, if <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>arg</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>Z</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>arg</mi><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>7</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>7</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> is (here <math><mi></mi><msup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>W</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>7</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>7</mn></mfrac></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>7</mn></mfrac><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>7</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>7</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>arg</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>W</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>7</mn></mfrac></math>

</details>

---

### Q51. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For a complex number <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math>, if the argument of <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mo>¯</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> or <math><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> (where <math><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo></math> <math><mi>b</mi></math> are two real numbers), then the value of <math><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></math> such that the locus of <math><mi>Z</mi></math> represents a circle with centre <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is (where, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>i</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let, <math><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>y</mi></math>, then
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mo>¯</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>Z</mi><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mo>¯</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mo>¯</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>Z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></math>
If the argument of the above complex number is <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> or <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>, then
<math><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>y</mi></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo></math> Centre of the circle is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo> </mo><mo>&amp;</mo><mo> </mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q52. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi></math> is a complex number, then the area of the triangle (in sq. units) whose vertices are the roots of the equation <math><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is equal to (where, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>i</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **2**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>i</mi><mo>,</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> sq. units

</details>

### Q53. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>Z</mi></math> be a complex number satisfying the relation <math><msup><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mo>&#175;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>. If the least possible argument of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math> is <math><mo>&#8211;</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>&#960;</mi></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi></math> is equal to (here, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>arg</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>Z</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0.6**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let, <math><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>r</mi><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>r</mi><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math> and <math><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> least <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">arg</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>6</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q54. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If the arguments of <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mo>¯</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> are equal, then the locus of <math><mi>Z</mi></math> is part of a circle with centre <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>. The value of  <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0.5**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let, <math><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>y</mi></math>
Now,
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mo>&#175;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>Z</mi><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mo>&#175;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="false"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow><mo>&#175;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mi>Z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>y</mi></math>
argument of <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p>Now, since both arguments are equal
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>where</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> <math><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Hence, the centre of the circle is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=0.</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q55. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The complex number <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi></math>, satisfying the equation <math><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></math> and <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">arg</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> simultaneously, is (where, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>i</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>i</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>i</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>i</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mi>i</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/c6bd758e-33d0-4875-b9e4-3c4d61ae77c3.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:2.53913in;height:1.57455in" />
<math><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> or <math><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>⇒</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>±</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>±</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
Only <math><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>i</mi></math> satisfies <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">arg</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q56. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math> is (where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math> is a complex number and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Re(</mi><mi>Z</mi><mo>)</mo></math> represents the real part of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math>)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0.75**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let, <math><mi>Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>y</mi></math></p><p> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>Z</mi></mfenced></mrow><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>Z</mi></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>7</mn><mn>5</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q57. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> satisfies the condition <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo> ≥</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>3</mn></math>. Then the least value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

From triangle inequality we know that <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>≥</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>

hence <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>≥</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>≥</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

Hence <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is the correct answer.

</details>

### Q58. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mtext>z</mtext><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mtext>, z</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mtext>z</mtext><mn>3</mn></msub></math> are three complex numbers such that <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>+</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>+</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><msub><mtext>z</mtext><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mtext>z</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mtext>z</mtext><mn>3</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </math> (given)

Now <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><msub><mtext>z</mtext><mn>1</mn></msub></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|"><msub><mtext>z</mtext><mn>1</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mtext>z</mtext><mn>1</mn></msub><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#175;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#175;</mo></mover></math>

Similarly, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#175;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>&#175;</mo></mover></math>

Now <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>+</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>+</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mover><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mrow><mo>&#175;</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>&#8757;</mo><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#175;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#175;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><mo>&#175;</mo></mover><mo>)</mo></math>

and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow><mo>|</mo><mover><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mrow><mo>&#175;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mrow><mo>(</mo><mo>&#8757;</mo><mrow><mo>|</mo><mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#175;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>|</mo></mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow></math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#8658;</mo> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>z</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </math>

</details>
